    WHERE (`p`.`StatusProduk` <> 'Tidak Aktif') OR `p`.`StatusProduk` IS NULL
    ORDER BY `p`.`Stok`
    LIMIT @__p_0
) AS `p4`
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT `p1`.`ProductUnitId`, `p1`.`ConversionValue`, `p1`.`HargaJual`, `p1`.`HargaModal`, `p1`.`IdProduk`, `p1`.`IsDeleted`, `p1`.`Is_Default`, `p1`.`UnitName`
    FROM `ProductUnits` AS `p1`
    WHERE NOT (`p1`.`IsDeleted`)
) AS `p5` ON `p4`.`IdProduk` = `p5`.`IdProduk`
ORDER BY `p4`.`Stok`, `p4`.`IdProduk`
